8*10/100/1000Base-T + 4*1G/2.5G/10GBase-X SFP+ L2+ Managed Industrial Boost PoE Switch with Temp-humidity Sensor

LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST

Download Annex

  • Overview
  • Features
  • Technical specification
  • Structure diagram
  • Order information

The SmartByte LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST is the highly reliable managed industrial boost PoE switch with 8-port 10/100/1000-T PoE and 4-port 1000/2500/10GBase-X fiber optical interfaces. It adopts DC 12-57V power boost technology to solve power source only 12-24V but require to support PoE, and no special power supplies needed. It transforms a limited 12-24V power source into a robust, standards-compliant PoE network, which can support IEEE802.3af, IEEE802.3at standard PoE protocol, the Max power consumption can reach 30W (PoE+) per port. It also supports ERPS redundant network and the self-recovery mechanism is less than 20ms on full load which allows you to scheme a reliable Ethernet network by building a redundant ring topology as your back-up solution. LT-IS6312GP-4GX8GT-D-BST supports DI/DO/RS485, it can work with temperature-humidity sensor to collect environmental data and trigger alarms, which is critical for the industrial application requiring the stable ambient conditions

 

LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST is the high cost-effective easy-to-use device, which provides essential industrial Ethernet networking function, including wide range power input 12-57VDC, redundant power design with polarity reverse protection, robust IP40 fan-less housing with Din-rail installation, wide operation temperature from -40°C to 75°C as well as high-level EMI/EMC capability. It is the best choice for mobile transportation & vehicle mounted systems, solar-powered & off-deployments and industrial environments with DC backplanes application scenarios

8*10/100/1000Base-T + 4*1G/2.5G/10GBase-X SFP+ L2+ Managed Industrial Boost PoE Switch with Temp-humidity Sensor

LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST

Attachment

Overview

The SmartByte LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST is the highly reliable managed industrial boost PoE switch with 8-port 10/100/1000-T PoE and 4-port 1000/2500/10GBase-X fiber optical interfaces. It adopts DC 12-57V power boost technology to solve power source only 12-24V but require to support PoE, and no special power supplies needed. It transforms a limited 12-24V power source into a robust, standards-compliant PoE network, which can support IEEE802.3af, IEEE802.3at standard PoE protocol, the Max power consumption can reach 30W (PoE+) per port. It also supports ERPS redundant network and the self-recovery mechanism is less than 20ms on full load which allows you to scheme a reliable Ethernet network by building a redundant ring topology as your back-up solution. LT-IS6312GP-4GX8GT-D-BST supports DI/DO/RS485, it can work with temperature-humidity sensor to collect environmental data and trigger alarms, which is critical for the industrial application requiring the stable ambient conditions

 

LT-IS6312XGP-4XS8GT-SD-BST is the high cost-effective easy-to-use device, which provides essential industrial Ethernet networking function, including wide range power input 12-57VDC, redundant power design with polarity reverse protection, robust IP40 fan-less housing with Din-rail installation, wide operation temperature from -40°C to 75°C as well as high-level EMI/EMC capability. It is the best choice for mobile transportation & vehicle mounted systems, solar-powered & off-deployments and industrial environments with DC backplanes application scenarios
